# LOSS_LEDGER -- zero-unnamed-losses accounting, 2026-08-12 framelogs

Track N4 of the overnight plan. Every hole in the clean-`host_seq` ladder of
every 2026-08-12 framelog under `two_jup/r3cap/` is classified below; the
unnamed bucket is enumerated event-by-event at the end. Generated by
`two_jup/loss_ledger.py` (this file is its output; re-run to regenerate).

## Method / class definitions

- A **loss event** = a gap in the sequence ladder of CRC-clean frames of the
  run's own `frames.bin` (dtype per `frame_taxonomy.read_frames`). Lost
  frames = sum of (gap-1). Direction per `meta.txt` `target=`/`rx=` (A/fwd:
  rx=10.0.0.148, B/rev: rx=10.0.0.146) -- verified for every run.
- **Empirical correction to the plan's cadence spec:** the boundary-event
  comb in these logs has period **~8 frames = 6.42 ms** (measured; spacing
  histogram dominated by 8/24/32-frame deltas with a slow +/-1 phase drift),
  i.e. HALF the -M16 area period the plan quotes -- and it stays ~8 frames
  at -M32 too. The test used: a k<=2 hole is *boundary* if its spacing to a
  neighboring small-hole event is within +/-1 frame (0.8 ms, inside the
  plan's +/-2 ms tolerance) of j*8 frames (j=1..6). Forward runs: either
  neighbor (comb density high, 86-95% of events lock). Reverse runs: BOTH
  neighbors required, because at reverse's low event density a one-sided
  match has ~37% chance-pass; two-sided ~14%.
- **tx-underrun-comb** (reverse only): spacing within +/-2 of j*33 frames
  (j=1..3) to a neighbor -- the TX zero-fill cadence of TX_ANOMALY_SCAN.md
  (revlong: 33.000-frame spacing). Takes precedence over the boundary test
  in reverse.
- **tx-mute-cand**: hole with a crc=0, host_seq==0 record inside or within
  +/-2 records (the SINGLES_REPLAY.md ~72 us mute signature), not comb-
  locked. Envelope verification not run (pair.iq covers only ~130 ms/run).
- **burst-frozen**: >20-frame hole with reg_packets frozen (dpk<=max(2,k/10)).
- **startup-burst**: >200-frame delivered-corrupt hole inside the first 15 s
  (the three ~1s corrupt bursts during bring-up, identical in every accept
  run; reg_packets advances, so NOT the frozen-burst class).
- **mid-gap**: 3-20 frames (its own class per plan; cadence flags kept).
- **feeder-gap** (singles_disc only, txlog_146.bin present): hole within
  +/-25 ms (wall-clock translated) of a TX submit gap >1.5 frame periods.
- Everything else: **unnamed**, enumerated below.
- Live window = accept_analyze.py's (settle 15 s, wedge-guarded live end);
  sum-check per run = my live-window lost total vs accept_analyze's miss.

**Unnamed total: 1845 events across all runs.**

## Notes and caveats

- evm_swap_B wedged at ~12 s (meta: MID_CAPTURE_WEDGE, 'NOT usable data');
  its rows cover the pre-wedge window only and the wedge truncation itself
  is a run-level event, not a hole (delivery flatlined; capture ended).
- accept_201153_r1 ran -M32 (detected from drain-burst structure and
  meta timestamps); all other runs -M16.
- The 8-frame comb finding (vs the plan's 12.85 ms area period) is the
  headline surprise; see Method above. The boundary class label is kept
  because the comb is still DMA-cadence-locked, but the period is half an
  area at -M16 and a QUARTER area at -M32.
- **Discovered structure #1 -- reverse 633-frame tick pairs:** in every
  reverse run the mid-gap/unnamed events organize into PAIRS: a k=2..9 hole
  followed by a k~2 hole exactly 633 frames (508 ms) later, with pair
  onsets repeating on a ~1.79 s / 3.58 s super-period (e.g. accept_155622:
  onsets 8.50, 12.15, 15.79, 23.01, 24.81, 28.39 s...). Events flagged in
  the 633-tick column; per-run flagged counts: accept_155622_r1=36, accept_160104_r1=32, accept_160104_r2=36, accept_160833_r1=4, accept_160833_r2=4, 135103_bud0_c1=33, 135103_bud0_c2=30, 135103_bud0_c3=26, 135103_bud4_c1=20, 135103_bud4_c2=16, 135103_bud4_c3=22, 151226_bud0_c1=52, 151226_bud0_c2=30, 151226_bud0_c3=40, 151226_bud4_c1=20, 151226_bud4_c2=28, 151226_bud4_c3=30
  This is the dominant REVERSE loss structure and is NOT in tonight's
  class list -- it stays mid-gap/unnamed here, named for the morning.
- **Discovered structure #2 -- bud0_c1 pre-settle alternating storm:** 443
  k=1 holes spaced exactly 2 frames apart (lost-clean-lost), ALL inside
  t=3.4-5.0 s (the third warm-up second, which in every other run is one
  contiguous startup-burst). Pre-settle, so excluded from the pooled
  live-window scoreboard; enumerated in the unnamed table (live=blank).
  Per-run k=1-with-2-frame-neighbor counts: accept_160833_r1=24, accept_160833_r2=25, accept_160833_r3=20, accept_201153_r1(M32)=51, 135103_bud0_c1=445, singles_disc=31, singles_reread=93, cp1_verdict2=35, evm_swap_A=23
- singles_disc txlog: 43 TX submit gaps (all ~1.2-1.3 ms
  = ~1.5 frame periods); holes matched within +/-25 ms: 0 classified feeder-gap.
